## Python-ecosystem inherited-surface CVEs (json/zip/tar/pickle/xml/file/path/tempfile/terminal/subprocess/os.system/JVM/compile/eval)

NLTK runs on CPython and shells out to external binaries / a JVM, so a CVE in any of these operation classes is in scope even when its record names a different product. Every hit is classified against NLTK's OWN surface; **nothing is dropped** — an absent surface is reported as N/A-by-absence, not omitted. Swept classes and their NVD result counts:
